| BUG-3002 | `missing-idempotency-key` | `app/services/invoice_payment_service.py` (`create_payment`; fix also in `app/api/v1/endpoints/invoice_payments.py` (header), `app/models` / `app/schemas/invoice_payment.py` + Alembic migration (column, partial unique index), `app/managers/invoice_payment_manager.py` (`create_payment`)) | The same payment sent twice is recorded twice | A double click, browser retry or lost response on `POST /invoice-payments/` saves a second identical payment if it still fits in what is owed: paid amount, status, audit row, event and order `paid` move twice; it can only be undone by voiding by hand | Owner (OQ-61, 2026-11-21, option A): optional `Idempotency-Key` header (`Header(min_length=1, max_length=100)`, 422 otherwise; compared exactly, case-sensitive) stored on the payment in `idempotency_key` `String(100)` with `idempotency_fingerprint` `String(64)` (both NULL when no key) under partial unique index `ix_invoice_payments_workspace_idempotency_key` ON `invoice_payments (workspace_id, idempotency_key) WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL`. Fingerprint = SHA-256 hex of canonical JSON of the caller's `profile_id` plus the normalised body (amount by value, `400` = `400.00`; text trimmed, omitted / empty = null). Lookup by `(workspace_id, key)` in the manager after the invoice `FOR UPDATE`, before the status / lock / amount checks, no lock of its own. Same key + same fingerprint → the first payment's response, nothing written or committed (the service rolls back), also after it was voided or the invoice became paid or locked; same key + different body **or another member** → 409 `This idempotency key was already used for a different payment`; an index violation is answered as replay or 409, never 500; no key → today's behaviour. The web app sends a fresh key per payment form. One rule with BUG-3023 (checkout) and the future portal (coordinator, 2026-11-21) | [spec Q3, create_payment R9–R9e](../../tests/unit/services/invoice_payment_service.spec.md) · [OQ-61](owner-questions.md) | open |
| BUG-3022 | `response-omits-stored-field` | `app/services/payment_transaction_service.py` (`_to_response`; fix in `app/schemas/payment_transaction.py` `PaymentTransactionInDB`; reached through `GET /payments/`, `GET /payments/{tran_id}/`, `POST /payments/{transaction_id}/resolve-risk/`) | Payment ledger does not show why a start was refused or whether an outcome was gateway-verified | `PaymentTransactionResponse` has neither `failed_reason` (column added by BUG-019) nor `outcome_verified` (column added by BUG-026); the list, detail and resolve-risk answers cannot show them | The response carries `failed_reason: str \| None` and `outcome_verified: bool` as stored on the row (best-practice default, delegated by reviewer, 2026-11-21) | [spec Q1, `_to_response` R1a](../../tests/unit/services/payment_transaction_service.spec.md) | open |
| BUG-3023 | `missing-idempotency-key` | `app/services/payment_transaction_service.py` (`initiate_payment`; also `app/managers/payment_transaction_manager.py`, `app/models/payment_transaction.py`, `POST /payments/initiate/` in `app/api/v1/endpoints/payments.py`) | Starting the same checkout twice opens two payable gateway sessions | Every call is a new attempt: a double click or retry makes a second `tran_id`, row and SSLCommerz session; both payment pages accept money | Owner (OQ-61, 2026-11-21), same pattern as BUG-3002: optional `Idempotency-Key` header (`Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key", min_length=1, max_length=100)`, 422 otherwise; case-sensitive, not trimmed) → new `idempotency_key` parameter; stored on the row with `idempotency_fingerprint` (`String(64)` NULL, SHA-256 hex of canonical JSON — `sort_keys=True`, `separators=(",", ":")` — over `profile_id` plus the normalised body: amount `format(amount.normalize(), "f")`, text trimmed, omitted/null/blank → null, case kept); both NULL without a key, never changed by later status updates; partial unique index `ix_payment_transactions_workspace_idempotency_key` on `(workspace_id, idempotency_key) WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL`. Key looked up (unlocked, `get_by_workspace_and_idempotency_key`) before the gateway call. Same key + same fingerprint → the stored attempt's `tran_id` / current `status` / `gateway_page_url` / `failed_reason`, 201, no gateway call, nothing written, ends with one rollback; same key + different body or another member → 409 `This idempotency key was already used for a different payment`; a race loser (its gateway session already opened, never exposed) gets `IntegrityError` on the index, rolls back, re-reads and answers as replay or 409, never 500; key in another workspace is unrelated; no key → today's behaviour. One rule with BUG-3002 and the future portal (coordinator, 2026-11-21) | [spec Q5, `initiate_payment` R7–R7e2](../../tests/unit/services/payment_transaction_service.spec.md) · [OQ-61](owner-questions.md) | open |
